No absolute weight min/max, no committee-weight normalization, and no cooldown period were invented (idempotency substitutes for the last). `confidence_calibration_score`'s missing aggregate formula was not invented — left unbuilt, does not block ROI-based V1 weighting. CLV persists `NULL` with zero effect on the calculation. The identified selection-bias feedback loop (higher weight → greater consensus influence → greater influence over which candidates become recommendations → those become future graded evidence) is documented, not claimed solved — V1's guardrails (200-observation minimum, 90-day window, ±10% cap, full fan-out participation regardless of weight, propose-only, append-only history) are safety controls, not mathematical proof.

  - **Root cause:** `cronSchedule: null` (v5.3.1's own "disable" mechanism) does not disable — Railway's own tool documentation states it makes a service "run continuously." Live deploy logs confirmed the dispatcher script actually executed and POSTed to `sports-intel-layer`'s real endpoint, failing only with a `404` because that service's own new route hadn't finished deploying in that exact moment — a timing accident, not a guarantee. Verified live twice, including immediately after the schedule-restore redeploy itself, which (per a further independent finding from the same investigation) also executed on deploy despite having a valid schedule — Railway runs a cron service's start command once immediately on every new deployment, not only at scheduled times, confirmed by direct observation on two separate deploys.

Reported to HQ per the explicit M3 instruction ("if the API contract lacks something required by the approved UI: STOP") rather than guessing at a shape. HQ classified this **Milestone 2.1 — Additive Product Grade Contract Correction** and authorized Option 1 (extend the existing read routes) — not a new endpoint, not a business-logic build.
- **2026-08-31 — Phase 6 Milestone 2.1: Additive Product Grade Contract Correction — COMPLETE [Volume 5 → v5.0.3].** Own separately-committed unit (`claude/phase6-m2-1-grade-contract`), M3 resumes from its merge.
  - **Built, api-gateway:** `app.recommendations` gained `_read_grade_events_by_product_ids` (batched, same `product_ids`-keyed pattern as every other context fetch already in this module) and `_current_grade` (pure resolution). `_serialize_product`/`_visible_products_with_context`/`get_recommendation_detail` all wired to attach `grade` (`null` when ungraded, else `{outcome, gradedAt, isCorrection, correctedAt}`), fetched only for already tier-authorized products — never for a product the caller isn't entitled to see.
  - **Current-outcome resolution reuses `app.track_record.get_track_record`'s already-established rule** (the append-only correction chain always appends a strictly-later `computed_at` row, so the latest row is authoritative) rather than inventing a second algorithm, per HQ's explicit instruction. `gradedAt` is the original grade event's `computed_at`; `correctedAt` is set only when the current row is itself a correction.
  - **Domain rule preserved exactly as HQ specified:** `recommendation_products.status` untouched, never carries `'graded'` — lifecycle status and grade state documented and tested as independent dimensions (a withdrawn product's `grade` may be non-null; an active product is very commonly `grade: null`).
  - **Per-leg grade exposure inspected and not added** — no field in Volume 5's approved card or four-layer detail contracts reads a per-leg outcome; `MIXED_SETTLED` is already the authoritative product-level value the API returns verbatim, kept minimal per HQ's explicit "no speculative fields" instruction.
  - **Full test/regression evidence:** 14 new tests (`test_recommendation_grade_contract.py` — null-when-ungraded, WIN/LOSS/PUSH/VOID_NO_ACTION/MIXED_SETTLED/NOT_APPLICABLE passthrough, correction-chain current-outcome + original-`gradedAt` preservation, lifecycle-status-never-mutated, withdrawn/grade independence, tier-gated 404 unchanged, list + today route inclusion, backward-compatibility field check). Existing `test_recommendations.py`/`test_recommendation_detail.py` extended with an empty-by-default grade-events mock to stay green. Full api-gateway suite: **79/79 passing** (65 pre-existing + 14 new). Relevant ai-orchestrator grading regression re-run for sanity (no ai-orchestrator code touched): `test_grading.py`/`test_postgame_grading.py`/`test_postgame_grading_persistence.py` — 32/32 passing, unaffected.
